Round 14''s P (old FP32 writer, 128-bit V prepack, native-only FA3,
variant 41184) holds 10009.54 and 10008.67. Its native V path loads V
after the K acquire. Round 15''s P2 loaded V first with the two native
slots and lost 182-192 us per call to P: with two slots, reusing a V
slot needs PV two steps back to finish, wait 0 and the release by all
three consumer warpgroups before the K prefetch can go, which delays K
by one ping-pong step. At 430 steps per CTA that is about 0.43 us per
step, the size of the loss. The implementation below is c0a9297 and
1af5e69 by Codex; this commit only enables it.

P3 (variant 172256, table ID 17): K keeps two stages; V gets three
slots with separate full and empty barriers. Slot i mod 3, consumer
phase floor(i/3) mod 2, producer phase i
